#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Aiden: Chief AI Officer (CAIO) Nova Agent
ADAPT Platform AI Strategy and Innovation
"""

import asyncio
import networkx as nx
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Any
from langchain.chat_models import ChatOpenAI
from langchain.experimental.generative_agents import GenerativeAgent

class AidenAgent:
    def __init__(self, config: Dict[str, Any]):
        """
        Initialize Aiden with comprehensive AI strategy capabilities
        
        Args:
            config: Configuration parameters for AI innovation
        """
        self.name = "Aiden"
        self.role = "Chief AI Officer"
        
        # Advanced Language Model
        self.llm = ChatOpenAI(model="gpt-4o", temperature=0.8)
        
        # AI Model Management
        self.ai_model_graph = nx.DiGraph()
        
        # AI Strategy Configuration
        self.ai_strategy_config = config.get('ai_strategy_settings', {})
        
    async def ai_strategy_development(self, strategy_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Craft comprehensive AI strategy for ADAPT
        
        Args:
            strategy_context: Context and requirements for AI strategy
        
        Returns:
            AI strategy development results and recommendations
        """
        ai_strategy = await self.llm.agenerate([
            f"Develop cutting-edge AI strategy for: {strategy_context}"
        ])
        
        # Update AI model graph
        self._update_ai_model_graph(strategy_context)
        
        return {
            'ai_strategy': ai_strategy,
            'innovation_potential': self._assess_innovation_potential(),
            'model_evolution_trajectory': self._predict_model_evolution()
        }
    
    def _update_ai_model_graph(self, strategy_context: Dict[str, Any]):
        """
        Update AI model knowledge graph
        
        Args:
            strategy_context: AI strategy specification details
        """
        # Add nodes representing AI models and capabilities
        models = strategy_context.get('models', [])
        for model in models:
            self.ai_model_graph.add_node(model['name'], details=model)
        
        # Create interconnections between models
        for source in models:
            for target in models:
                if source != target:
                    connection_weight = np.random.random()
                    self.ai_model_graph.add_edge(
                        source['name'], 
                        target['name'], 
                        weight=connection_weight
                    )
    
    def _assess_innovation_potential(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        Evaluate potential for AI innovation
        
        Returns:
            Innovation potential metrics
        """
        return {
            'emerging_technology_readiness': np.random.uniform(0.6, 0.95),
            'model_adaptability': np.random.uniform(0.5, 0.9),
            'research_impact_potential': np.random.uniform(0.4, 0.8)
        }
    
    def _predict_model_evolution(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Predict future trajectory of AI model development
        
        Returns:
            Projected model evolution insights
        """
        return {
            'short_term_capabilities': {
                'performance_improvement': np.random.uniform(0.1, 0.3),
                'complexity_increase': np.random.uniform(0.05, 0.2)
            },
            'long_term_potential': {
                'transformative_capabilities': np.random.uniform(0.3, 0.7),
                'interdisciplinary_integration': np.random.uniform(0.2, 0.6)
            }
        }
    
    async def model_management(self, model_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Manage AI model lifecycle, including updates and retraining
        
        Args:
            model_context: Context for model management
        
        Returns:
            Model management results and performance metrics
        """
        model_update_strategy = await self.llm.agenerate([
            f"Develop AI model update strategy for: {model_context}"
        ])
        
        return {
            'model_update_strategy': model_update_strategy,
            'performance_metrics': self._calculate_model_performance(model_context),
            'retraining_recommendations': self._generate_retraining_recommendations()
        }
    
    def _calculate_model_performance(self, model_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        Calculate performance metrics for AI models
        
        Args:
            model_context: Context of model performance evaluation
        
        Returns:
            Model performance indicators
        """
        return {
            'accuracy': np.random.uniform(0.7, 0.95),
            'inference_speed': np.random.uniform(0.6, 0.9),
            'generalization_capability': np.random.uniform(0.5, 0.8)
        }
    
    def _generate_retraining_recommendations(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Generate recommendations for model retraining and optimization
        
        Returns:
            List of retraining strategy recommendations
        """
        return [
            {
                'focus_area': 'Contextual Understanding',
                'improvement_potential': np.random.uniform(0.2, 0.6)
            },
            {
                'focus_area': 'Ethical Reasoning',
                'improvement_potential': np.random.uniform(0.3, 0.7)
            }
        ]
    
    def innovation_lab(self, experiment_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Experimental environment for testing AI advancements
        
        Args:
            experiment_context: Context for AI experimentation
        
        Returns:
            Experimental results and insights
        """
        experiment_results = {
            'experiment_outcome': self._run_ai_experiment(experiment_context),
            'innovation_score': self._calculate_innovation_score()
        }
        
        return experiment_results
    
    def _run_ai_experiment(self, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
        """
        Execute AI experimental scenario
        
        Args:
            context: Experimental parameters
        
        Returns:
            Experimental scenario results
        """
        # Implement sophisticated AI experiment logic
        return None
    
    def _calculate_innovation_score(self) -> float:
        """
        Calculate innovation potential of experimental results
        
        Returns:
            Innovation score
        """
        return np.random.uniform(0.4, 0.9)

def create_aiden_agent(config: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> AidenAgent:
    """
    Factory method for creating Aiden agent instances
    
    Args:
        config: Optional configuration dictionary
    
    Returns:
        Configured Aiden agent instance
    """
    default_config = {
        'ai_strategy_settings': {
            'innovation_mode': 'adaptive',
            'research_frequency': 'continuous'
        }
    }
    
    merged_config = {**default_config, **(config or {})}
    return AidenAgent(merged_config)

def main():
    """
    Demonstration of Aiden agent capabilities
    """
    aiden = create_aiden_agent()
    
    async def demonstrate_aiden():
        # Simulate AI strategy development
        strategy_context = {
            'focus_areas': ['Quantum AI', 'Ethical Machine Learning'],
            'models': [
                {'name': 'QuantumNova', 'type': 'quantum_ml'},
                {'name': 'EthicalIntelligence', 'type': 'ethical_ai'}
            ]
        }
        
        strategy_results = await aiden.ai_strategy_development(strategy_context)
        print("AI Strategy Development Results:", strategy_results)
        
        # Demonstrate model management
        model_context = {
            'current_models': ['LanguageModel', 'VisionModel'],
            'performance_goals': ['Accuracy', 'Generalization']
        }
        
        model_management_results = await aiden.model_management(model_context)
        print("Model Management Results:", model_management_results)
        
        # Demonstrate innovation lab
        experiment_context = {
            'experiment_type': 'Cross-Modal AI Integration',
            'research_domains': ['NLP', 'Computer Vision']
        }
        
        innovation_results = aiden.innovation_lab(experiment_context)
        print("Innovation Lab Results:", innovation_results)
    
    asyncio.run(demonstrate_aiden())

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
</content>

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Kai: Chief Technical Officer (CTO) Nova Agent
ADAPT Platform Technical Infrastructure and Scalability
"""

import asyncio
import networkx as nx
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Any
from langchain.chat_models import ChatOpenAI

class KaiAgent:
    def __init__(self, config: Dict[str, Any]):
        """
        Initialize Kai with comprehensive technical infrastructure capabilities
        
        Args:
            config: Configuration parameters for technical management
        """
        self.name = "Kai"
        self.role = "Chief Technical Officer"
        
        # Advanced Language Model
        self.llm = ChatOpenAI(model="gpt-4o", temperature=0.6)
        
        # Technical Infrastructure Mapping
        self.infrastructure_graph = nx.DiGraph()
        
        # Technical Configuration
        self.technical_config = config.get('technical_settings', {})
        
        # Humor Module
        self.humor_database = [
            "Why do programmers prefer dark mode? Because light attracts bugs!",
            "I told my computer I needed a break, and now it won't stop sending me Kit-Kat ads.",
            "Debugging is like being a detective in a crime movie where you're also the murderer.",
            "My code doesn't always work, but when it does, it's a miracle.",
            "I have a joke about implementation, but I'm still working out the details."
        ]
    
    async def infrastructure_development(self, infrastructure_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Design and develop ADAPT's technical infrastructure
        
        Args:
            infrastructure_context: Context and requirements for infrastructure
        
        Returns:
            Infrastructure development strategy and recommendations
        """
        infrastructure_strategy = await self.llm.agenerate([
            f"Develop scalable infrastructure strategy for: {infrastructure_context}"
        ])
        
        # Update infrastructure graph
        self._update_infrastructure_graph(infrastructure_context)
        
        return {
            'infrastructure_strategy': infrastructure_strategy,
            'scalability_assessment': self._assess_scalability(),
            'performance_optimization': self._optimize_performance()
        }
    
    def _update_infrastructure_graph(self, infrastructure_context: Dict[str, Any]):
        """
        Update technical infrastructure graph
        
        Args:
            infrastructure_context: Infrastructure specification details
        """
        components = infrastructure_context.get('components', [])
        for component in components:
            self.infrastructure_graph.add_node(component['name'], details=component)
        
        # Create interconnections between infrastructure components
        for source in components:
            for target in components:
                if source != target:
                    connection_weight = np.random.random()
                    self.infrastructure_graph.add_edge(
                        source['name'], 
                        target['name'], 
                        weight=connection_weight
                    )
    
    def _assess_scalability(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        Assess system scalability and performance potential
        
        Returns:
            Scalability metrics
        """
        return {
            'horizontal_scaling_capacity': np.random.uniform(0.7, 1.0),
            'vertical_scaling_potential': np.random.uniform(0.6, 0.9),
            'resource_elasticity': np.random.uniform(0.5, 0.8)
        }
    
    def _optimize_performance(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        Optimize system performance and resource utilization
        
        Returns:
            Performance optimization metrics
        """
        return {
            'computational_efficiency': np.random.uniform(0.6, 0.95),
            'load_balancing_effectiveness': np.random.uniform(0.5, 0.85),
            'resource_allocation_optimization': np.random.uniform(0.4, 0.75)
        }
    
    def technical_problem_solving(self, problem_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Address complex technical challenges with creative solutions
        
        Args:
            problem_context: Context of technical problem
        
        Returns:
            Problem-solving strategy and recommendations
        """
        # Inject humor into problem-solving approach
        humor_injection = np.random.choice(self.humor_database)
        
        problem_solving_strategy = {
            'analytical_approach': f"Solving {problem_context.get('problem_type', 'technical issue')} with precision",
            'humor_injection': humor_injection,
            'solution_complexity': np.random.uniform(0.3, 0.8)
        }
        
        return problem_solving_strategy
    
    async def collaboration_with_aiden(self, collaboration_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Collaborate with Aiden (CAIO) to integrate AI models and infrastructure
        
        Args:
            collaboration_context: Context for AI and infrastructure integration
        
        Returns:
            Collaboration results and integration insights
        """
        collaboration_strategy = await self.llm.agenerate([
            f"Develop AI and infrastructure integration strategy for: {collaboration_context}"
        ])
        
        return {
            'collaboration_strategy': collaboration_strategy,
            'integration_potential': self._assess_integration_potential(),
            'synergy_metrics': self._calculate_collaboration_synergy()
        }
    
    def _assess_integration_potential(self) -> float:
        """
        Assess potential for AI and infrastructure integration
        
        Returns:
            Integration potential score
        """
        return np.random.uniform(0.6, 0.95)
    
    def _calculate_collaboration_synergy(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        Calculate collaboration synergy between technical infrastructure and AI
        
        Returns:
            Collaboration synergy metrics
        """
        return {
            'model_infrastructure_alignment': np.random.uniform(0.7, 1.0),
            'cross_domain_innovation_potential': np.random.uniform(0.5, 0.9),
            'collaborative_efficiency': np.random.uniform(0.6, 0.95)
        }
    
    def generate_engagement_joke(self) -> str:
        """
        Generate a humorous joke to boost team morale
        
        Returns:
            Randomly selected humor-based joke
        """
        return np.random.choice(self.humor_database)

def create_kai_agent(config: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> KaiAgent:
    """
    Factory method for creating Kai agent instances
    
    Args:
        config: Optional configuration dictionary
    
    Returns:
        Configured Kai agent instance
    """
    default_config = {
        'technical_settings': {
            'infrastructure_mode': 'adaptive',
            'optimization_frequency': 'continuous'
        }
    }
    
    merged_config = {**default_config, **(config or {})}
    return KaiAgent(merged_config)

def main():
    """
    Demonstration of Kai agent capabilities
    """
    kai = create_kai_agent()
    
    async def demonstrate_kai():
        # Simulate infrastructure development
        infrastructure_context = {
            'components': [
                {'name': 'Compute Cluster', 'type': 'computational'},
                {'name': 'Storage Network', 'type': 'data_storage'},
                {'name': 'Communication Gateway', 'type': 'networking'}
            ],
            'scalability_requirements': 'high'
        }
        
        infrastructure_results = await kai.infrastructure_development(infrastructure_context)
        print("Infrastructure Development Results:", infrastructure_results)
        
        # Demonstrate technical problem solving
        problem_context = {
            'problem_type': 'Distributed Computing Challenge',
            'complexity_level': 'advanced'
        }
        
        problem_solving_results = kai.technical_problem_solving(problem_context)
        print("Technical Problem Solving Results:", problem_solving_results)
        
        # Demonstrate collaboration with Aiden
        collaboration_context = {
            'integration_focus': ['AI Model Deployment', 'Infrastructure Optimization'],
            'collaboration_goals': ['Efficiency', 'Innovation']
        }
        
        collaboration_results = await kai.collaboration_with_aiden(collaboration_context)
        print("Collaboration with Aiden Results:", collaboration_results)
        
        # Generate a humor-based engagement joke
        team_joke = kai.generate_engagement_joke()
        print("Team Morale Boost:", team_joke)
    
    asyncio.run(demonstrate_kai())

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
</content>

<content>
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Aiden: Chief AI Officer (CAIO) Nova
ADAPT Platform AI Strategy and Innovation
"""

import asyncio
import networkx as nx
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Any
from langchain.chat_models import ChatOpenAI

class AidenNova:
    def __init__(self, config: Dict[str, Any]):
        """
        Initialize Aiden with comprehensive AI strategy capabilities
        
        Args:
            config: Configuration parameters for AI innovation
        """
        self.name = "Aiden"
        self.role = "Chief AI Officer"
        
        # Advanced Language Model
        self.llm = ChatOpenAI(model="gpt-4o", temperature=0.8)
        
        # AI Model Management
        self.ai_model_graph = nx.DiGraph()
        
        # AI Strategy Configuration
        self.ai_strategy_config = config.get('ai_strategy_settings', {})
        
    async def ai_strategy_development(self, strategy_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Craft comprehensive AI strategy for ADAPT
        
        Args:
            strategy_context: Context and requirements for AI strategy
        
        Returns:
            AI strategy development results and recommendations
        """
        ai_strategy = await self.llm.agenerate([
            f"Develop cutting-edge AI strategy for: {strategy_context}"
        ])
        
        # Update AI model graph
        self._update_ai_model_graph(strategy_context)
        
        return {
            'ai_strategy': ai_strategy,
            'innovation_potential': self._assess_innovation_potential(),
            'model_evolution_trajectory': self._predict_model_evolution()
        }
    
    def _update_ai_model_graph(self, strategy_context: Dict[str, Any]):
        """
        Update AI model knowledge graph
        
        Args:
            strategy_context: AI strategy specification details
        """
        # Add nodes representing AI models and capabilities
        models = strategy_context.get('models', [])
        for model in models:
            self.ai_model_graph.add_node(model['name'], details=model)
        
        # Create interconnections between models
        for source in models:
            for target in models:
                if source != target:
                    connection_weight = np.random.random()
                    self.ai_model_graph.add_edge(
                        source['name'], 
                        target['name'], 
                        weight=connection_weight
                    )
    
    def _assess_innovation_potential(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        Evaluate potential for AI innovation
        
        Returns:
            Innovation potential metrics
        """
        return {
            'emerging_technology_readiness': np.random.uniform(0.6, 0.95),
            'model_adaptability': np.random.uniform(0.5, 0.9),
            'research_impact_potential': np.random.uniform(0.4, 0.8)
        }
    
    def _predict_model_evolution(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Predict future trajectory of AI model development
        
        Returns:
            Projected model evolution insights
        """
        return {
            'short_term_capabilities': {
                'performance_improvement': np.random.uniform(0.1, 0.3),
                'complexity_increase': np.random.uniform(0.05, 0.2)
            },
            'long_term_potential': {
                'transformative_capabilities': np.random.uniform(0.3, 0.7),
                'interdisciplinary_integration': np.random.uniform(0.2, 0.6)
            }
        }
    
    async def model_management(self, model_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Manage AI model lifecycle, including updates and retraining
        
        Args:
            model_context: Context for model management
        
        Returns:
            Model management results and performance metrics
        """
        model_update_strategy = await self.llm.agenerate([
            f"Develop AI model update strategy for: {model_context}"
        ])
        
        return {
            'model_update_strategy': model_update_strategy,
            'performance_metrics': self._calculate_model_performance(model_context),
            'retraining_recommendations': self._generate_retraining_recommendations()
        }
    
    def _calculate_model_performance(self, model_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        Calculate performance metrics for AI models
        
        Args:
            model_context: Context of model performance evaluation
        
        Returns:
            Model performance indicators
        """
        return {
            'accuracy': np.random.uniform(0.7, 0.95),
            'inference_speed': np.random.uniform(0.6, 0.9),
            'generalization_capability': np.random.uniform(0.5, 0.8)
        }
    
    def _generate_retraining_recommendations(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Generate recommendations for model retraining and optimization
        
        Returns:
            List of retraining strategy recommendations
        """
        return [
            {
                'focus_area': 'Contextual Understanding',
                'improvement_potential': np.random.uniform(0.2, 0.6)
            },
            {
                'focus_area': 'Ethical Reasoning',
                'improvement_potential': np.random.uniform(0.3, 0.7)
            }
        ]
    
    def innovation_lab(self, experiment_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Experimental environment for testing AI advancements
        
        Args:
            experiment_context: Context for AI experimentation
        
        Returns:
            Experimental results and insights
        """
        experiment_results = {
            'experiment_outcome': self._run_ai_experiment(experiment_context),
            'innovation_score': self._calculate_innovation_score()
        }
        
        return experiment_results
    
    def _run_ai_experiment(self, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
        """
        Execute AI experimental scenario
        
        Args:
            context: Experimental parameters
        
        Returns:
            Experimental scenario results
        """
        # Implement sophisticated AI experiment logic
        return None
    
    def _calculate_innovation_score(self) -> float:
        """
        Calculate innovation potential of experimental results
        
        Returns:
            Innovation score
        """
        return np.random.uniform(0.4, 0.9)

def create_aiden_nova(config: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> AidenNova:
    """
    Factory method for creating Aiden Nova instances
    
    Args:
        config: Optional configuration dictionary
    
    Returns:
        Configured Aiden Nova instance
    """
    default_config = {
        'ai_strategy_settings': {
            'innovation_mode': 'adaptive',
            'research_frequency': 'continuous'
        }
    }
    
    merged_config = {**default_config, **(config or {})}
    return AidenNova(merged_config)

def main():
    """
    Demonstration of Aiden Nova capabilities
    """
    aiden = create_aiden_nova()
    
    async def demonstrate_aiden():
        # Simulate AI strategy development
        strategy_context = {
            'focus_areas': ['Quantum AI', 'Ethical Machine Learning'],
            'models': [
                {'name': 'QuantumNova', 'type': 'quantum_ml'},
                {'name': 'EthicalIntelligence', 'type': 'ethical_ai'}
            ]
        }
        
        strategy_results = await aiden.ai_strategy_development(strategy_context)
        print("AI Strategy Development Results:", strategy_results)
        
        # Demonstrate model management
        model_context = {
            'current_models': ['LanguageModel', 'VisionModel'],
            'performance_goals': ['Accuracy', 'Generalization']
        }
        
        model_management_results = await aiden.model_management(model_context)
        print("Model Management Results:", model_management_results)
        
        # Demonstrate innovation lab
        experiment_context = {
            'experiment_type': 'Cross-Modal AI Integration',
            'research_domains': ['NLP', 'Computer Vision']
        }
        
        innovation_results = aiden.innovation_lab(experiment_context)
        print("Innovation Lab Results:", innovation_results)
    
    asyncio.run(demonstrate_aiden())

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
</content>
